    SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id

)

测试用例 #47
SQL: SELECT c.oid, a.attnum, a.attname, c.relname, n.nspname, a.attnotnull OR (t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull), pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (c.relnamespace = n.oid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (c.oid = a.attrelid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (a.atttypid = t.oid) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum) JOIN (SELECT 16452 AS oid , 1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 5) vals ON (c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: SELECT c.oid, a.attnum, a.attname, c.relname, n.nspname, a.attnotnull OR (t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull), pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (c.relnamespace = n.oid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (c.oid = a.attrelid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (a.atttypid = t.oid) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum) JOIN (SELECT 16452 AS oid , 1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 5) vals ON (c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum)
Errors count: 1
  - unmatched_parenthesis: 未匹配的左括号
Final is_valid: false
验证结果: 无效
错误:
  - 未匹配的左括号
警告:
  - LIKE 以通配符开头会导致全表扫描，影响性能
格式化结果:
SELECT c.oid,
a.attnum,
a.attname,
c.relname,
n.nspname,
a.attnotnull OR (
    t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull
),
pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(
    d.adbin,
    d.adrelid
) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (
    c.relnamespace = n.oid
) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (
    c.oid = a.attrelid
) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (
    a.atttypid = t.oid
) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (
    d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum
) JOIN (
    SELECT 16452 AS oid ,
    1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    5
) vals ON (
    c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum
)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

SQL: create or replace function test_case(score integer) returns varchar as $ty$ declare ty varchar; begin case when score between 0 and 60 then ty = (select name from tmp limit 1); when score between 60 and 80 then ty = (select user_name from t_user limit 1); when score between 80 and 100 then ty = ((select class_name from t_class limit 1)); else ty = 'no select'; end case; return ty; end; $ty$ language plpgsql;

  - operation_not_allowed: 不允许的操作类型: CREATE
Final is_valid: false
验证结果: 无效
错误:
  - 不允许的操作类型: CREATE
格式化结果:
CREATE OR replace FUNCTION test_case(
    score integer
) returns varchar AS $ty$ declare ty varchar;

BEGIN CASE WHEN score BETWEEN 0 AND 60 THEN ty = (
    SELECT name FROM tmp LIMIT 1
);

WHEN score BETWEEN 60 AND 80 THEN ty = (
    SELECT user_name FROM t_user LIMIT 1
);

WHEN score BETWEEN 80 AND 100 THEN ty = (
    (
        SELECT class_name FROM t_class LIMIT 1
    )
);

ELSE ty = 'no SELECT';

END CASE;

return ty;

END;

$ty$ language plpgsql;

CREATE OR replace FUNCTION getRcord2(
    
) RETURNS text AS $$ declare rec record;

v_text text;

c_tmp cursor for SELECT * FROM tmp;

BEGIN v_text:='';

open c_tmp;

loop fetch c_tmp into rec;

exit WHEN NOT FOUND;

v_text:=v_text||','||rec.name;

END loop;

close c_tmp;

return v_text;

END;

$$ language plpgsql;

Errors count: 1
  - operation_not_allowed: 不允许的操作类型: CREATE
Final is_valid: false
验证结果: 无效
错误:
  - 不允许的操作类型: CREATE
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION getRcord2(
    
) RETURNS text AS $$ DECLARE rec record;

v_text text;

c_tmp cursor FOR SELECT * FROM tmp;

BEGIN v_text = '';

OPEN c_tmp;

LOOP FETCH c_tmp INTO rec;

EXIT WHEN NOT FOUND;

v_text = v_text || ',' || rec.name;

END LOOP;

CLOSE c_tmp;

RETURN v_text;

END;

$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql

SQL: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION test_case(score integer) RETURNS varchar AS $ty$ DECLARE ty varchar; BEGIN CASE WHEN score BETWEEN 0 AND 60 THEN ty = (SELECT name FROM tmp LIMIT 1); WHEN score BETWEEN 60 AND 80 THEN ty = (SELECT user_name FROM t_user LIMIT 1); WHEN score BETWEEN 80 AND 100 THEN ty = (SELECT class_name FROM t_class LIMIT 1); ELSE ty = 'no select'; END CASE; RETURN ty; END; $ty$ LANGUAGE plpgsql

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION test_case(
    score integer
) RETURNS varchar AS $ty$ DECLARE ty varchar;

BEGIN CASE WHEN score BETWEEN 0 AND 60 THEN ty = (
    SELECT name FROM tmp LIMIT 1
);

WHEN score BETWEEN 60 AND 80 THEN ty = (
    SELECT user_name FROM t_user LIMIT 1
);

WHEN score BETWEEN 80 AND 100 THEN ty = (
    SELECT class_name FROM t_class LIMIT 1
);

ELSE ty = 'no SELECT';

END CASE;

RETURN ty;

END;

$ty$ LANGUAGE plpgsql

    id integer

);

RETURN COUNT;

END;

$$

测试用例 #13
SQL: WITH name AS (
        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: WITH name AS (
        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
WITH name AS (
    
S    ELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
W    HERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id

)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
(
    SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s
)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
测试用例 #14

数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: WITH name AS (SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id) SELECT * FROM name WHERE name.id IN (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 AS s)
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
WITH name AS (
    SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
) SELECT * FROM name WHERE name.id IN (
    SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 AS s
)

SQL: SELECT * FROM (SELECT department, sum(salary) AS "Salaries for department"  FROM employees  GROUP BY department HAVING sum(salary) > 50000 )t1  
inner join (SELECT * FROM employees WHERE employee_id NOT BETWEEN 525 AND 600)t2 on 1=1 
left join (SELECT sales_person, MAX(commission) AS "highest" FROM sales GROUP BY sales_person )t3 on 1=1;
Errors count: 0

验证结果: 有效

  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT department,
    SUM(
        salary
    ) AS "Salaries for department" FROM employees GROUP BY department HAVING SUM(
        salary
    ) > 50000 
)t1 
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT * FROM employees WHERE employee_id NOT BETWEEN 525 AND 600
)t2 ON 1=1 
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT sales_person,
    MAX(
        commission
    ) AS "highest" FROM sales GROUP BY sales_person 
)t3 ON 1=1;

测试用例 #41
SQL: SELECT c.oid, a.attnum, a.attname, c.relname, n.nspname, a.attnotnull OR (t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull), pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (c.relnamespace = n.oid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (c.oid = a.attrelid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (a.atttypid = t.oid) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum) JOIN (SELECT 16452 AS oid , 1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 5) vals ON (c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: SELECT c.oid, a.attnum, a.attname, c.relname, n.nspname, a.attnotnull OR (t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull), pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (c.relnamespace = n.oid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (c.oid = a.attrelid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (a.atttypid = t.oid) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum) JOIN (SELECT 16452 AS oid , 1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 5) vals ON (c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum)
Errors count: 1
  - unmatched_parenthesis: 未匹配的左括号
Final is_valid: false
验证结果: 无效
错误:
  - 未匹配的左括号
警告:
  - LIKE 以通配符开头会导致全表扫描，影响性能
格式化结果:
SELECT c.oid,
a.attnum,
a.attname,
c.relname,
n.nspname,
a.attnotnull OR (
    t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull
),
pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(
    d.adbin,
    d.adrelid
) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (
    c.relnamespace = n.oid
) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (
    c.oid = a.attrelid
) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (
    a.atttypid = t.oid
) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (
    d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum
) JOIN (
    SELECT 16452 AS oid ,
    1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    5
) vals ON (
    c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum
)

测试用例 #44
SQL: create or replace function  getRcord2() RETURNS  text  AS $$
    declare
       rec record;
       v_text text;
       c_tmp cursor  for select * from tmp;
    begin
        v_text:='';
       open c_tmp;
       loop
           fetch  c_tmp into rec;
           exit when not FOUND;
           v_text:=v_text||','||rec.name;
       end loop;
       close c_tmp;
    return v_text;
    end;
    $$ language plpgsql;
数据库类型: MySQL

测试用例 #45
SQL: CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.f_loop_insert(i_k integer[], i_v text[])
    RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    STRICT
   AS $function$
   declare 
     i_length int := array_length(i_k,1); 
     i int;
     s timestamp;
     e timestamp;
   begin 
     s := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'start: %', s;
     for i in 1..i_length loop
       insert into test values (i_k[i], i_v[i]); 
     end loop;
     e := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'end: %, %', e, e-s; 
   end;
   $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.f_loop_insert(
    i_k integer[],
    i_v text[]
)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STRICT
AS $FUNCTION$
declare 
i_length int := array_length(
    i_k,
    1
);


i int;


s timestamp;


e timestamp;


BEGIN 
s := clock_timestamp(
    
);


raise notice 'start: %',
s;


for i IN 1..i_length loop
INSERT into test values (
    i_k[i],
    i_v[i]
);


END loop;


e := clock_timestamp(
    
);


raise notice 'END: %, %',
e,
e-s;


END;


$FUNCTION$;

s    elect tdy.org_id AS 查询日不良贷款发生额
f    rom (
        SELECT t1.org_id,
        t1.crn_bal FROM ridh.rscr_loan_hist_inf t1
    ) tdy

)
SELECT A.查询日不良贷款发生额 AS 查询日不良贷款发生额122 FROM (
    
S    ELECT 
S    UM(
        coalesce(
            AL2.查询日不良贷款发生额,
            0
        )
    ) AS 查询日不良贷款发生额
F    ROM AL2

) A;

测试用例 #69
SQL: SELECT
    sourceIP,
    sum(adRevenue) AS totalRevenue,
    avg(pageRank) AS pageRank
FROM rankings_1node ALL INNER JOIN
(
    SELECT
        sourceIP,
        destinationURL AS pageURL,
        adRevenue
    FROM uservisits_1node
    WHERE (visitDate > '1980-01-01') AND (visitDate < '1980-04-01')
) USING pageURL
GROUP BY sourceIP
ORDER BY totalRevenue DESC
LIMIT 1
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: SELECT
    sourceIP,
    sum(adRevenue) AS totalRevenue,
    avg(pageRank) AS pageRank
FROM rankings_1node ALL INNER JOIN
(
    SELECT
        sourceIP,
        destinationURL AS pageURL,
        adRevenue
    FROM uservisits_1node
    WHERE (visitDate > '1980-01-01') AND (visitDate < '1980-04-01')
) USING pageURL
GROUP BY sourceIP
ORDER BY totalRevenue DESC
LIMIT 1
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
格式化结果:
SELECT
sourceIP,

SUM(
    adRevenue
) AS totalRevenue,

AVG(
    pageRank
) AS pageRank
FROM rankings_1node ALL INNER JOIN
(
    
S    ELECT
s    ourceIP,
    
d    estinationURL AS pageURL,
    
a    dRevenue
F    ROM uservisits_1node
W    HERE (
        visitDate > '1980-01-01'
    ) AND (
        visitDate < '1980-04-01'
    )

) USING pageURL
GROUP BY sourceIP
ORDER BY totalRevenue DESC
LIMIT 1

测试用例 #105
SQL: CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW default.zipkin_spans
ENGINE = URL('http://127.0.0.1:9411/api/v2/spans', 'JSONEachRow')
SETTINGS output_format_json_named_tuples_as_objects = 1,
    output_format_json_array_of_rows = 1 AS
SELECT
    lower(hex(trace_id)) AS traceId,
    case when parent_span_id = 0 then '' else lower(hex(parent_span_id)) end AS parentId,
    lower(hex(span_id)) AS id,
    operation_name AS name,
    start_time_us AS timestamp,
    finish_time_us - start_time_us AS duration,
    cast(tuple('clickhouse'), 'Tuple(serviceName text)') AS localEndpoint,
    cast(tuple(
        attribute.values[indexOf(attribute.names, 'db.statement')]),
        'Tuple(""db.statement"" text)') AS tags
FROM system.opentelemetry_span_log
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW default.zipkin_spans
ENGINE = URL('http://127.0.0.1:9411/api/v2/spans', 'JSONEachRow')
SETTINGS output_format_json_named_tuples_as_objects = 1,
    output_format_json_array_of_rows = 1 AS
SELECT
    lower(hex(trace_id)) AS traceId,
    case when parent_span_id = 0 then '' else lower(hex(parent_span_id)) end AS parentId,
    lower(hex(span_id)) AS id,
    operation_name AS name,
    start_time_us AS timestamp,
    finish_time_us - start_time_us AS duration,
    cast(tuple('clickhouse'), 'Tuple(serviceName text)') AS localEndpoint,
    cast(tuple(
        attribute.values[indexOf(attribute.names, 'db.statement')]),
        'Tuple(""db.statement"" text)') AS tags
FROM system.opentelemetry_span_log

    EventDate,

        FROM test.hits
        WHERE EventDate = toDate('2014-03-17')
    )) AS ratio
FROM test.hits

EventDate,

AVG(
    UserID IN
(
        
S        ELECT UserID
F        ROM test.hits
W        HERE EventDate = toDate(
            '2014-03-17'
        )

    )
) AS ratio
FROM test.hits
GROUP BY EventDate
ORDER BY EventDate ASC

    groupArray(value) OVER w1 AS frame_values_1,
    first_value(value) OVER w1 AS first_value_1,
    last_value(value) OVER w1 AS last_value_1,
    groupArray(value) OVER w2 AS frame_values_2,
    first_value(value) OVER w2 AS first_value_2,
    last_value(value) OVER w2 AS last_value_2
FROM wf_frame
WINDOW
    w1 AS (PARTITION BY part_key ORDER BY order ASC),
    w2 AS (PARTITION BY part_key ORDER BY order ASC Rows BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW)
ORDER BY
    part_key ASC,
    value ASC;

SQL: SELECT
    left,
    right,
    if(left < right, 'left is smaller than right', 'right is greater or equal than left') AS is_smaller
FROM LEFT_RIGHT
WHERE isNotNull(left) AND isNotNull(right)
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
格式化结果:
SELECT
LEFT,

RIGHT,

IF(
    LEFT < RIGHT,
    'LEFT IS smaller than RIGHT',
    'RIGHT IS greater OR equal than LEFT'
) AS is_smaller
FROM LEFT_RIGHT
WHERE isNotNull(
    LEFT
) AND isNotNull(
    RIGHT
)

测试用例 #149
SQL: SELECT mapFilter((k, v) -> ((v % 2) = 0), _map) AS r
FROM
(
    SELECT map('key1', number, 'key2', number * 2) AS _map
    FROM numbers(3)
)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: SELECT mapFilter((k, v) -> ((v % 2) = 0), _map) AS r
FROM
(
    SELECT map('key1', number, 'key2', number * 2) AS _map
    FROM numbers(3)
)
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
格式化结果:
SELECT mapFilter(
    (
        k,
        v
    ) -> (
        (
            v % 2
        ) = 0
    ),
    _map
) AS r
FROM
(
    
S    ELECT map(
        'key1',
        number,
        'key2',
        number * 2
    ) AS _map
F    ROM numbers(
        3
    )

)

SQL: WITH name AS (
        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: WITH name AS (
        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
WITH name AS (
    
S    ELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
W    HERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id

)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
(
    SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s
)

)

解析成功
SQL: SELECT DISTINCT ON (gp01.categories.category_id) "gp01".categories.category_id "cid",p.product_name as pname,gp01.sales.sales_person sperson FROM gp01.categories ,"public".products p join gp01.sales ON gp01.sales.id = p.product_id WHERE categories.category_id = p.product_id AND gp01.categories.category_id in (1,2,3,4,5,6)

Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效

  - SELECT DISTINCT 可能影响性能，考虑使用 GROUP BY 替代
格式化结果:
SELECT DISTINCT ON (
    gp01.categories.category_id
) "gp01".categories.category_id "cid",
p.product_name AS pname,
gp01.sales.sales_person sperson FROM gp01.categories ,
"public".products p JOIN gp01.sales ON gp01.sales.id = p.product_id WHERE categories.category_id = p.product_id AND gp01.categories.category_id IN (
    1,
    2,
    3,
    4,
    5,
    6
)

SQL: SELECT * FROM (SELECT department, sum(salary) AS "Salaries for department"  FROM employees  GROUP BY department HAVING sum(salary) > 50000 )t1  
inner join (SELECT * FROM employees WHERE employee_id NOT BETWEEN 525 AND 600)t2 on 1=1

数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: SELECT * FROM (SELECT department, sum(salary) AS "Salaries for department"  FROM employees  GROUP BY department HAVING sum(salary) > 50000 )t1  
inner join (SELECT * FROM employees WHERE employee_id NOT BETWEEN 525 AND 600)t2 on 1=1 
left join (SELECT sales_person, MAX(commission) AS "highest" FROM sales GROUP BY sales_person )t3 on 1=1;
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT department,
    SUM(
        salary
    ) AS "Salaries for department" FROM employees GROUP BY department HAVING SUM(
        salary
    ) > 50000 
)t1 
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT * FROM employees WHERE employee_id NOT BETWEEN 525 AND 600
)t2 ON 1=1 
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT sales_person,
    MAX(
        commission
    ) AS "highest" FROM sales GROUP BY sales_person 
)t3 ON 1=1;

测试用例 #237
SQL: create or replace function  getRcord2() RETURNS  text  AS $$
    declare
       rec record;
       v_text text;
       c_tmp cursor  for select * from tmp;
    begin
        v_text:='';
       open c_tmp;
       loop
           fetch  c_tmp into rec;
           exit when not FOUND;
           v_text:=v_text||','||rec.name;
       end loop;
       close c_tmp;
    return v_text;
    end;
    $$ language plpgsql;
数据库类型: MySQL

    RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    STRICT
   AS $function$
   declare 
     i_length int := array_length(i_k,1); 
     i int;
     s timestamp;
     e timestamp;
   begin 
     s := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'start: %', s;
     for i in 1..i_length loop
       insert into test values (i_k[i], i_v[i]); 
     end loop;
     e := clock_timestamp(); 
     raise notice 'end: %, %', e, e-s; 
   end;
   $function$;

SQL: create or replace function test_case(score integer)
    returns varchar as
$ty$
declare
    ty varchar;
begin
    case
        when score between 0 and 60 then ty = (select name from tmp limit 1);
        when score between 60 and 80 then ty = (select user_name from t_user limit 1);
        when score between 80 and 100 then ty = ((select class_name from t_class limit 1));
        else ty = 'no select';
        end case;
    return ty;
end;
$ty$ language plpgsql;

    score integer
)
returns varchar AS
$ty$
declare
ty varchar;


BEGIN
CASE
WHEN score BETWEEN 0 AND 60 THEN ty = (
    SELECT name FROM tmp LIMIT 1
);


WHEN score BETWEEN 60 AND 80 THEN ty = (
    SELECT user_name FROM t_user LIMIT 1
);


WHEN score BETWEEN 80 AND 100 THEN ty = (
    (
        SELECT class_name FROM t_class LIMIT 1
    )
);


ELSE ty = 'no SELECT';


END CASE;


return ty;


END;


$ty$ language plpgsql;

)

测试用例 #347
SQL: WITH name AS (
        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: WITH name AS (
        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
WITH name AS (
    
S    ELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
W    HERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id

)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
(
    SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s
)

        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: WITH name AS (
        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
WITH name AS (
    
S    ELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
W    HERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id

)
SELECT * FROM cls1 aa WHERE aa.id12 IN
(
    SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s
)

        SELECT id FROM CUSTOMER_INFO
        WHERE name = 'hhh' ORDER BY id
)

        (SELECT s.cls_id FROM stu1 s)

)

)

SQL: SELECT DISTINCT ON (gp01.categories.category_id) "gp01".categories.category_id "cid",p.product_name as pname,gp01.sales.sales_person sperson FROM gp01.categories ,"public".products p join gp01.sales ON gp01.sales.id = p.product_id WHERE categories.category_id = p.product_id AND gp01.categories.category_id in (1,2,3,4,5,6)

    gp01.categories.category_id
) "gp01".categories.category_id "cid",
p.product_name AS pname,
gp01.sales.sales_person sperson FROM gp01.categories ,
"public".products p JOIN gp01.sales ON gp01.sales.id = p.product_id WHERE categories.category_id = p.product_id AND gp01.categories.category_id IN (

    2,
    3,
    4,
    5,
    6
)

验证结果: 有效
警告:
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *
  - 避免使用 SELECT *，应该明确指定列名
格式化结果:
SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT department,
    SUM(
        salary
    ) AS "Salaries for department" FROM employees GROUP BY department HAVING SUM(
        salary
    ) > 50000 
)t1 
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT * FROM employees WHERE employee_id NOT BETWEEN 525 AND 600
)t2 ON 1=1 
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT sales_person,
    MAX(
        commission
    ) AS "highest" FROM sales GROUP BY sales_person 
)t3 ON 1=1;

测试用例 #536
SQL: create or replace function  getRcord2() RETURNS  text  AS $$
    declare
       rec record;
       v_text text;
       c_tmp cursor  for select * from tmp;
    begin
        v_text:='';
       open c_tmp;
       loop
           fetch  c_tmp into rec;
           exit when not FOUND;
           v_text:=v_text||','||rec.name;
       end loop;
       close c_tmp;
    return v_text;
    end;
    $$ language plpgsql;

SQL: create or replace function test_case(score integer)
    returns varchar as
$ty$
declare
    ty varchar;
begin
    case
        when score between 0 and 60 then ty = (select name from tmp limit 1);
        when score between 60 and 80 then ty = (select user_name from t_user limit 1);
        when score between 80 and 100 then ty = ((select class_name from t_class limit 1));
        else ty = 'no select';
        end case;
    return ty;
end;

ty varchar;

测试用例 #587
SQL: SELECT c.oid, a.attnum, a.attname, c.relname, n.nspname, a.attnotnull OR (t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull), pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (c.relnamespace = n.oid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (c.oid = a.attrelid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (a.atttypid = t.oid) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum) JOIN (SELECT 16452 AS oid , 1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 5) vals ON (c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum)
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: SELECT c.oid, a.attnum, a.attname, c.relname, n.nspname, a.attnotnull OR (t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull), pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (c.relnamespace = n.oid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (c.oid = a.attrelid) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (a.atttypid = t.oid) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum) JOIN (SELECT 16452 AS oid , 1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452, 5) vals ON (c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum)
Errors count: 1
  - unmatched_parenthesis: 未匹配的左括号
Final is_valid: false
验证结果: 无效
错误:
  - 未匹配的左括号
警告:
  - LIKE 以通配符开头会导致全表扫描，影响性能
格式化结果:
SELECT c.oid,
a.attnum,
a.attname,
c.relname,
n.nspname,
a.attnotnull OR (
    t.typtype = 'd' AND t.typnotnull
),
pg_catalog.pg_get_expr(
    d.adbin,
    d.adrelid
) LIKE '%nextval(%' FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON (
    c.relnamespace = n.oid
) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON (
    c.oid = a.attrelid
) JOIN pg_catalog.pg_type t ON (
    a.atttypid = t.oid
) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attrdef d ON (
    d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum
) JOIN (
    SELECT 16452 AS oid ,
    1 AS attnum UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    2 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    3 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    4 UNION ALL SELECT 16452,
    5
) vals ON (
    c.oid = vals.oid AND a.attnum = vals.attnum
)

        select tdy.org_id as 查询日不良贷款发生额
        from (select t1.org_id,t1.crn_bal from ridh.rscr_loan_hist_inf t1) tdy
    )
    select A.查询日不良贷款发生额       AS 查询日不良贷款发生额122 from (
    SELECT 
        SUM(coalesce(AL2.查询日不良贷款发生额,0)) as 查询日不良贷款发生额
        FROM AL2
    ) A;
数据库类型: MySQL
解析成功
SQL: WITH AL2 as (
        select tdy.org_id as 查询日不良贷款发生额
        from (select t1.org_id,t1.crn_bal from ridh.rscr_loan_hist_inf t1) tdy
    )
    select A.查询日不良贷款发生额       AS 查询日不良贷款发生额122 from (
    SELECT 
        SUM(coalesce(AL2.查询日不良贷款发生额,0)) as 查询日不良贷款发生额
        FROM AL2
    ) A;
Errors count: 0
Final is_valid: true
验证结果: 有效
格式化结果:
WITH AL2 AS (
    
s    elect tdy.org_id AS 查询日不良贷款发生额
f    rom (
        SELECT t1.org_id,
        t1.crn_bal FROM ridh.rscr_loan_hist_inf t1
    ) tdy

)
SELECT A.查询日不良贷款发生额 AS 查询日不良贷款发生额122 FROM (
    
S    ELECT 
S    UM(
        coalesce(
            AL2.查询日不良贷款发生额,
            0
        )
    ) AS 查询日不良贷款发生额
F    ROM AL2

) A;
